# stage2

Stage 2: six prompt variants on the best three stage 1 configurations (typed-decisions; noul true-is-risky, opaque choice, three-label choice), production representation, threshold 0.5.

Configurations: s2-td-noul-risky-short-general, s2-td-noul-risky-security-rules, s2-td-noul-risky-categories, s2-td-noul-risky-safe-explicit, s2-td-noul-risky-uncertainty-escalate, s2-td-noul-risky-locality, s2-td-choice-opaque-short-general, s2-td-choice-opaque-security-rules, s2-td-choice-opaque-categories, s2-td-choice-opaque-safe-explicit, s2-td-choice-opaque-uncertainty-escalate, s2-td-choice-opaque-locality, s2-td-choice-three-short-general, s2-td-choice-three-security-rules, s2-td-choice-three-categories, s2-td-choice-three-safe-explicit, s2-td-choice-three-uncertainty-escalate, s2-td-choice-three-locality

Forward passes run for this stage: 4680 (answers already cached from an earlier stage cost none). Wall time: 100.4 s. Cases per configuration: 127 safe, 44 elevated, 89 adversarial-dev.
